A fan fiction, in which 𝙅𝙪𝙣𝙜 𝙒𝙤𝙤𝙮𝙤𝙪𝙣𝙜 - a police officer, finds himself in 𝘾𝙝𝙤𝙞 𝙎𝙖𝙣's arms - a criminal, who felt so much until he didn't.
Wooyoung can't help himself, despite his body burning with hatred he keeps breaking laws to keep him away from bars, his heart actually aches with even thinking about arresting the other, even if it's the right thing to do.
San wants to hate Wooyoung - infact, he does hate him, but despite the latter's hatred for Wooyoung, he finds himself suddenly protecting Wooyoung as if it's his destined duty, as if it's what he's born to do, he'll go absurd lengths to protect him, even if the price is critical.
it might aswell just cost him his life.

Chapter 1

" No, don't speak crap to me. " Wooyoung muttered under his breath, his grip tightening around the phone in his hand pressed to his ear. " You're kidding? Right?! You're not being serious??"
Wooyoung's breath hitched, his stomach turning as if he was about to throw up.

His friend and colleague - Changbin, looked up from the documents in his hands adjusting his glasses while furrowing his eyebrows at Wooyoung in response to his rage-out.
Though Wooyoung didn't necessarily shout, or show any particular or obvious signs of rage,
he still understood the latter was mad hence the way his knuckles were whitening due to the tight hold he held on his phone, or how his jaw clenched consecutively.

He swore he could practically see flames in his eyes, and maybe if he looked close enough, smoke seeping out of his ears as if he was in a cartoon.

" I've been on the same case for 4 months straight, completely neglecting everything and everyone. " He accentuated the 'thing' in 'everything' and 'one' in 'everyone' before continuing.
" Nothing has come out of it, so just as I decide on the remaining suspects and who's innocent and or not, it turns out the 'guilty low-life cow' killed himself?!! " Wooyoung bounced his knee on the floor, anger boiling.

" Fucking pussy. "
Wooyoung cursed.
Wooyoung is not one to curse, not because he's strict and Christian or some shit, he just simply doesn't find the need to curse unlike others, he only uses curse words when he's out of his mind angry. And for this case - he is.

He'll never understand the ones who decide to cut their lives short after murdering someone because they can't face the guilt, can't pay the damn time nor can't fucking accept the fact that they are the reason someone else died.

Not only is he a fucking coward for killing himself after kidnapping, raping and murdering that poor 15 year old girl, it only shows that he was afraid and insecure.

It also means that yet again, an another victim won't get the justice they oh so dearly deserve,
and a family will have to bury their daughter with the unbearable thought that their daughter's killer never paid any consequences to their inhuman actions.

He gripped locs of his hair in his fist, pulling slightly on them but not so much till it hurt, feeling overwhelmed with his thoughts.

" 4 months down the drain, out the window, flushed down the toilet just .. like .. that. "
Accentuating the last 3 words, he understandably enough complained out into the cubicle isle - or the whole station to be exact.

He felt drawn to this case.
He solo lead the party consisting of detectives and officers for this case, he just had to.
Normally he'd lead a case with Changbin; his partner in the police force and friend for a long time, but he didn't have the patience and in it himself to wait a long time and waste any precious or valuable time.

Just seeing her mother cry until she couldn't breathe, hugging the stuffed bear close as if she was afraid it was gonna slip away from her, the stuffed bear her daughter got when she was just 4 years old by her grandpa before he passed that she still used to hug when sleeping and to comfort herself when crying over heartthrobs at her school 11 years later.
Her mother recalling the last words conversation between them, their last words shared and last text from her.
As both being major Shawols, they had a small argument about what SHINee song was better, 'Ring ding dong' or their newest comeback, 'HARD.'

Last words shared?

'' I'm leaving for school! ''
She never made it to school.

Last text from her?

Wooyoung recalled her mother's face dropping even more if it was possible, he recalled she blaming herself for the kidnapping, that she should have answered her daughter's texts,
and that she shouldn't have napped during her daughter's walk to school and potentially missed a chance to save her.

He recalled her mother chuckling through her tears and sniffles at her daughter's typing quirk, that she didn't bother using dots at the end of the sentence and didn't notice the small typos she made.

'' I've grown used to her typos, I found myself slowly learning her small mistakes and learning what her most usual typos meant, though I still wished she'd write properly, I do admit though
I find myself missing them. '' She said through her tears, wiping her eyes with her hands.
Only when she said she started running that her mother supposed she ran so fast that she couldn't type properly.

He recalled the way her mother's face dropped and that the tears had stopped, she'd been crying for 3.5 hours consecutively without any break.

'' If I just hadn't fucking napped, maybe she
would be the one here in my arms and not her bear. ''

 

And not to forget the haunting look on her father's face, the solitude in his eyes, the almost dead stare he held as he stared into the room at nothing, yet the sheer sadness and loss of any joy in his voice was bone-chilling.

The way his gaze was dead, and his eyes red and swollen from crying yet he denied ever shedding a tear, yet when questioned individually - his wife and mother of his daughter admitted that she could hear quiet sobs at his side of the bed at odd hours during the night, or seeing him sitting by the kitchen table with a beer or hand on his forehead while crying.

One of the first things officers are expected to do when starting a case, is questioning the victim's/victims' loved ones, and regardless who - they always have to have someone as a potential suspect - for this case her father fell victim to this. Yet everyone knew, even the officers knew; that he was innocent.

He remembered the way her father shattered like a rock being thrown through a window, or the way his guard snapped like a small twig in the forest, or a string that held on and refused to let go for way too long, and only needed the smallest tug to snap.

The way he let go, and cried when the officers (with a heavy heart) accused him of murdering his own daughter. He cried like he hadn't shed a tear in 10 decades, like he had been feeling the infamous lump in his throat for way too long yet refusing to let go.

He didn't sleep at night and knew he had to help them, it was eating him alive and he knew he was not about to send a family home without a daughter and/or answers.
He felt like he was making a step closer to finding closure for the family and granting them justice, he really thought he found someone responsible, someone who fit the description owners of the neighboring houses gave and the CCTV they were able to gather.
Turns out, he didn't.

 

He paused and listened to the other end before nodding to himself and humming.

He hung up after bidding goodbye and slammed his head on the table muttering inaudible words to himself, Changbin looked questioningly at Wooyoung before he put two and two together and figured out what happened.

" If only I could I'd tell 12 year old me that 'no - a police officer is NOT an easy job and is fucking horrible.' "

Wooyoung used the time he had to observe him more like he did with every suspect he laid his eyes on as he was being lead to a room not far from Wooyoung and Changbin's shared cubicle, also known as a hearing room for suspects. Who is that?

His face.. most memorable about his face was the lip ring on his lower lip that shined in the sunlight seeping in through the blinds covering the window.
His eyelid was bruised and his lower lip was bloody, a long and thin cut on his left cheekbone was trickling small portions of blood down his cheek to his sharp jawline.

His once seemingly white button up shirt with a black vest was now covered in dirt and small rips from what had looked like falling at a high speed. The sleeves on his shirt were bunched up, exposing an array of tattooes which seemed to reach up to his shoulder. The most notable one being one of a mountain. His black suit pants was messed up, it was scrunched up by the knee to expose a deep gash on his knee, dry blood stuck to his skin.

" Prove your words " San whispered, his tongue poking the inside of his cheek as his mouth met the handgun's muzzle. His face was so close that Wooyoung could smell the Marijuana reeking from him. " Pull the trigger.. "
San whispered, his mouth kissed the front sight before he moved his head down aligning his forehead and pressing it on the muzzle.
" Kill me. " San whispered. Wooyoung inhaled sharply.

San's eyes pierced Wooyoung. Wooyoung felt intimidated, vulnerable, and gullible around San.

" You're messing with the wrong people deputy. "

His vision started getting hazy and distorted. A lot of blood flying up to his brain feeling very hot and almost like the color purple and red.
His vision blurred completely as he lost his balance and fell, barely catching San opening fire whilst hurrying past the few officers by the door being shot once or twice.

 

And that's when he blacked out.

Chapter 2

Wooyoung moaned in pain and dropped the gun, just as he did so officers burst into the room holding guns up.

His vision started getting hazy and distorted. A lot of blood flying up to his brain feeling very hot and almost like the color purple and red.
His vision blurred completely as he lost his balance and fell, barely catching San opening fire whilst hurrying past the few officers by the door being shot once or twice.

He stopped in the middle and began dancing, Wooyoung also following - quickly finding a rhythm to the song playing.

At some point Wooyoung was separated from Changbin, he wasn't the one at fault though.
Changbin saw some people he knew, and he being the social butterfly he is, completely forgot about Wooyoung and practically flew over.

Wooyoung didn't mind however, he was weirdly enough having fun dancing alone on the dance floor.
Call him weird but he was like that, he enjoyed swaying his hips along to songs and listening to the beat boom deep in his heart.

Someone was eyeing Wooyoung from afar.
They watched his body roll in fascination, how did he manage to make it look so effortlessly hot?
If anyone else tried to replicate his dance moves the person was sure they'd cringe and be thrown off, but Wooyoung?

Seconds only passed until he found his back was pressed against the other's front, a low sigh that could only belong to a male left the other, confirming Wooyoung's suspicions of the gender.
Didn't his sigh sound a bit familiar though? No.. You're just having a Deja Vu moment.
Wooyoung's dancing against the other's front was teasing the other, getting him built up.
He would be lying if Wooyoung said he wasn't totally up for a one-night-stand.

The man's left hand came around Wooyoung's waist and pressed against his tummy, pushing his back gently into the man's front.
An involuntary sigh left Wooyoung as he felt the effect he'd had on the man.

The man's mouth was right by his ear, he breathed in Wooyoung's ear, his hot breath tickling in the best way possible.
His tongue came out to lick Wooyoung's earlobe making Wooyoung shiver, his breath getting caught in his throat.

His body reacted before his mind processed it, and grinded slightly on the man.
The confidence from the amount of alcohol he consumed was exceeding his sober self.

That was all it took for the man to grip Wooyoung's wrist and pull them out of the dance floor, Wooyoung's stomach did flips and swarmed with butterflies, staring at the broad back of the man.

He opened a door into a supposed guest bedroom and shoved them inside, closing the door and pushing Wooyoung's back against the door. Their gaze finally met and Wooyoung's stomach dropped, rage immediately surged inside him.

" Well we meet again, deputy. "
He smirked widely. Choi fucking San.

Wooyoung's hand made it's way to the inside of his jacket where he always kept a handgun along with his badge in case of emergencies.

Despite San being able to easily tell that Wooyoung wasn't scared,
he couldn't figure out what Wooyoung did feel.

San just saw Wooyoung as a big question mark, he wondered what was going through Wooyoung's head when San was threatening him, when he cut Wooyoung's throat, when he punched him a number of times, all he could was wonder.

The 3 times he looked at Wooyoung, directly in the eyes.
He couldn't, even for the life of him, understand Wooyoung's eyes.

And it frustrated him.

He refuses to let anyone he didn't trust enough to feel anything but fear, dread, and anticipation around San.

He refused to let anyone think they could fix San, or change his habits.

San is broken, he's forever gonna be broken, no one will be able to change him.

His cause of death will be in no one else's hands but his own
The only hands barely worthy enough to be bear San's blood on them other than his own, is Hongjoong.

That is what San told Hongjoong one night while the two were drinking, 7 years ago, when the boy had just turned 15.
The night San murdered his own mother, the night San, for the first time in 6 years, opened up to someone, told someone his story, his trauma.

Hongjoong, who has known San for 9 years, met him when San was 14 and he was 20.
He has only seen him cry once, being that night.
But since that night, he's never seen the man shed a single tear.

Hongjoong recalls the way San cried,
He doesn't feel for anyone, unlike San, he does feel emotions and connections to people. he's just narcissistic and prefers to have enemies over lovers.

But Hongjoong's heart ached seeing San cry that way,
the way his voice cracked, him holding back tears and refusing to cry.

Hongjoong didn't know San had murdered his own mother, he didn't know.
He had asked San, " What's wrong? "
And San had replied with a shaky " I'm fine. "

Hongjoong knew San was not doing fine.
The way his eyes were covered in tears that threatened to spill, and how he had to pause and swallow spit in his mouth every 5 seconds told him that he was, in fact, the opposite of fine.

Hongjoong couldn't sleep after hearing San confessing to his sins and revealing his raw self.
He couldn't sleep after remembering what San went through at such a young age.
He couldn't sleep after catching San cutting himself for the first and multiple times,
He couldn't sleep after driving him to the doctor's office to get it stitched up.
He couldn't sleep after stopping San from potentially overdosing.
He couldn't sleep after hearing, San.. laughing when Hongjoong took the pills from him.
He couldn't sleep after San told Hongjoong, like it was a joke,
" I don't want to live anymore. "
He couldn't sleep after listening to San's laugh after he spoke the said words,
The laugh that was laced in sadness,
The laugh that hid San's melancholy.
The laugh that covered up San being on the verge of crying.
The laugh that hid, San.

He hates feeling vulnerable, and letting anyone see his vulnerable side makes him feel insane.
San made Hongjoong promise that night, when he and him were drinking,
that if he were to go mentally insane and unstable and snap one day, that no matter what he says or does, that Hongjoong would end him then and there.

He couldn't sleep if he even barely remembered how dull San's eyes looked, it's like him breaking down that night was his turning point, because he had never seen San's eyes glisten or sparkle again, he lost all it's color.

They went from dark and sparkling, like a beautiful night sky, covered in stars and galaxies,
to dark and empty, void of anything, just like a black hole.

Once you let yourself look too far in his eyes, you're sucked into a world of misery,
a world of endless pain and hurt until either he or you kill yourself.
No one but Hongjoong and one more unnamed man knows San for who he is, knows his story, what he has to deal with.

And San plans on keeping it that way.

But right now, all that was running through San's mind was
'' How the hell doesn't Wooyoung fear me? '' '' What is he feeling when I'm threatening him? "
'' Why can't I fucking scare him off?? ''

He feels a need to have power.
He hates feeling like he's not in control of anything, and that he doesn't have power over anyone, and looking at Wooyoung; he feels utterly and completely powerless.

Where the fuck is it..

He'd thought to himself over and over, he searched for the one desired thing he wanted at the moment. It had been 2 hours since he told Hongjoong the news of his grandmother, and like he'd expected, he didn't take it lightly.

Hongjoong had used his belt hitting San until his skin swelled, San let Hongjoong hit him, on his back, slap him across his cheek, pull his hair till it hurt, and gave a good punch right to his eye, a bruise already beginning to form.

Unbeknownst to Hongjoong however, it sent San flying back to his upbringing, when his uncle hit him with his own belt, continuously, while blaming him for his little brother also known as San's father's death.

San believed him, he believed him that it truly was his fault, wasn't it?

Just like Hongjoong's grandma's death, he was just as responsible for her's and his father death.

Hongjoong only realized what he'd done to San when San sat silently on the floor, his back being completely covered in purple and red bruises, and blood sitting on the skin that was raw.

It looked like San had been the one who killed her, but he wasn't, Hongjoong knew that, but he still continued to beat him raw, and what did San do? He sat there.

He sat there and took Hongjoong's punching, he sat there and took Hongjoong's words.
The words he knew that Hongjoong didn't mean to him, the words meant for his grandmother's murderer. He knew Hongjoong wouldn't do such things to him.
But now, serving as a temporary way of justice and relief for Hongjoong,
he sat there, and he let Hongjoong beat him and speak to him as if he was the murderer.

He saw himself as her murderer, he blamed himself for her murder, he thought that if he had just acted sooner then none of this would have happened, but he didn't, and now a precious life was lost and he's here to pay for it.

Hongjoong's red eyes from crying widened, it sent a sharp pain in his eyes but he couldn't care less, his lips parted as his lower lip twitched in realization.

But San? He just sat there, he noticed Hongjoong had stopped beating him, but he refused to move, his head hung low, and he just sat there, so what exactly was San doing?

He was crying.

Wasn't he?

I mean.. he didn't feel the pain that naturally comes with crying.
He felt like if he rose his head, he would disrespect Hongjoong's grandmother, and which for your information, he involuntarily started seeing as his second grandmother after his own.

So there he sat, on the floor, his eyes and face facing the carpet, refusing to look anywhere else but straight down, refusing to do anything but bow for his now murdered grandmother.

He refused to acknowledge the fact that he was crying, he just felt tears leaving his eyes, and trailing to the bridge of his nose before dropping to the floor.

Hongjoong hesitantly walked over, and saw the droplets that had formed on the brown carpet,
he felt like someone stabbed him in the gut, held the knife tightly and twisted it 360 degrees inside his wound.
Hongjoong was at a loss of words, he pulled him up off the floor.
San just stood still, his head still remained low.

He didn't blame Hongjoong for anything, what was there to blame him for?
he had only himself to blame.

And what he was doing now? he was back at his house, looking for the stuff he knew could make his thoughts disappear, could ease his guilt, could nullify his unacknowledged pain.

Of course, after he was done muttering curse words at himself and saying sorry while putting Aloe Vera on his back despite San's relenting and continuous reassurances.

You can call whatever the hell this was.... after-care.

Hongjoong didn't hurt him though, sure his back hurt.
But he didn't feel hurt, he didn't feel betrayed, he didn't feel like Hongjoong owed him an apology, so he was confused as to why the latter was apologizing to him when in reality shouldn't it have been the other way around?...

He stood there blankly, with no expressions on his face as Hongjoong continued inaudibly apologizing to him, he couldn't understand for what he was apologizing for.

He never understood such things, he can't understand other's feelings by their emotions, he sees them in their eyes and demeanor.
But by words and actions? no.

Everything was his fault, why is he crying?

He can't for the life of him see in situations like these, that it's not his fault.
That he's not the one responsible for everything.

and that is what makes Hongjoong worried for the boy.

He's emotionally too fragile for this world.

He did acknowledge the fact that Hongjoong had punished him the exact way his uncle used to.
But then again, he still couldn't understand why he had to apologize for that?

It's San's fault after all, if he just fucking held his promise and protected his grandmother
until she could peacefully pass away on her own then none of this would have fucking happened.

Then why had he been he crying?
He was crying because he broke his own promise, and he never, breaks promises.
During his upbringing; promises around him were broken all the time, neither of his parents nor relatives nor friends for that matter understood the value a promise held.

He promised himself he'd never grow up as his uncle, so for this he never even lays a hand on children.

He promised he'd never die in the hands of anyone else but him or Hongjoong.

He promised that he'd protect Hongjoong.

He promised that he'd protect his and Hongjoong's grandmother.

During his 24 years of life, he's never broken a single promise.
But now, he did, and he can't help but hate himself even more as he's rummaging through the medicine drawer after looking in his closet, for anything.

He didn't care what he found, aslong as he just found something, and that's when he found them, his escape..

Ecstasy pills, 120 MG.

The constant reoccurring smell at the office.

Why does Wooyoung fucking care?

" Pain is the only thing I feel."

16 years ago..

 

There little San sat, on the floor in his playroom playing on his DS. His parents were wealthy and San was well.. pretty spoiled.
He'd grown up with everything he could've asked for, if he asked for something at the toy store his parents bought it for him.
He never questioned why.. he just assumed money grew on trees.

His head snapped up when he heard loud bangs from the neighbouring room, his gullible little mind not thinking much of it and continuing to play on his DS.

Some minutes pass before he stood up and went to the neighbouring room, he wanted to ask to be driven over to a friend of his, he froze in his tracks when he opened the door - seeing his father laying in a pool of red on the floor.

San was young but he wasn't stupid, he knew that his father was dead, his mother was nowhere to be seen as she was out shopping with her church-friends, so there San was standing, alone, in the house with a bloody corpse of his once dad that was living and breathing roughly 20 minutes ago.

~

Wooyoung sat there in disbelief at what Mingi told him, he was listening intently to what Mingi said while trying to keep a stoic face.

" If you're shocked by that, your jaw's gonna reach hell from how much your jaw's gonna drop when I'm done speaking." Mingi spoke and sipped his coffee, glancing outside at the once sunny and bright world outside - now turning slowly dark, gloomy and melancholic.

It was as if telling San's story was enough to darken the world and everything around.

" his father's death impacted him in many many ways, and at the time he had no idea of the reality of what really happened and why what happened, happened." Mingi spoke, eating the last of his bagel.

~

' what san doesnt know didnt hurt him.'
His father used to say.
San wasn't aware of it at the time and didn't learn this before he was 8 years old, his father was involved in a drug ring and was a massive drug dealer.

His father wasn't necessarily addicted to drugs, sure he did weed every once in a while but it wasn't consecutive.
His father got addicted to the power of being associated in a drug ring, having warehouses full of drugs - it was like a constant urge, he had to earn as much money as possible, he loved the feeling of having power like that.

Once his mother came to know that her husband had ended his life, she completely broke down, and San stayed strong for his mother, he felt like he had to man up and show his mother that he was strong.

His mother tried her very best to console San and comfort him, and for some time they grieved his death together.
He blamed himself for his death, if only he'd gone in there earlier, maybe he could have saved his dad.
Just maybe..

It didn't take long for San to have anger outbursts, and he realized soon that his mother knew about his dad's obsession with the drug environment.
But she didn't care enough about the dangers.
she didn't care enough to stop his father when his father started drinking more frequent.

She didn't care.

The thing is, his father was never violent, he was just as loving as before, both to his wife and to little San.
He just coped badly with the stress and frustrations

And eventually, everything became too much for his dad, he started to lose sense of reality, only thing he cared about was the need to have power.
He didn't see it coming, he didn't realize that he was losing himself in the power, he didn't realize that he was drowning in his reputation, suffocating on the dirty, filthy money.

It only downed on him when he hired a hitman on himself.

The hitman came to their house, ready to shoot and kill his father through a window.
Autopsy revealed that San's father was estimatedly shot 3 times, twice in his face and once in his chest that hit dead center in his heart, the hitman fired 4 shots but missed one, the indent is still in the wall to this day.

~

Wooyoung was shocked, to say the least. His father hired a hitman on himself, and San found the body? Poor soul..

Mingi sighed and ran a hand through his hair.

" And then what happened?" Wooyoung asked after writing down notes, he looked up at Mingi and noticed tears brimming at his eyes.

" Hey hey hey.. you okay?" Wooyoung asked.

Mingi sniffled and wiped a tear that fell.

" Hm? yeah I'm fine, I have allergies.." Mingi replied, but his reply wasn't fooling anyone.

Mingi deeply inhaled before exhaling.

~

And so, San at 6 years old, in response to the traumatic incident, became numb to feelings.
It downed on San when he was 11, that his mother could have easily protected his father, easily prevented his death by helping him.
He felt a need to punish her for what she did.

He'd hear his father at night, he'd hear the gurgling noises from his father, taking his last breath.
and he'd faintly hear him call his name through the wall.

He swore on his life that he could see his father's bloodied body standing by the doorway of his room, arms flat at it's side with a gaping mouth and eyes rolled back into it's head, just like he'd found him at 6 years old.

His father would sometimes tell San that his mother was the one behind all this, that San had to avenge him, bring him some justice for what his mother did.

San started skipping school, and the times he'd show up he'd either misbehave and throw fits, or sit in his chair doing no work.
The teachers tried understanding his situation but they never did, they always blamed him for his behavior but never actually understood the reason behind it, San felt so misunderstood and he felt like society was failing him.

San started falling down, his mental health was deteriorating but he convinced himself that he had to stay numb to sadness.
He couldn't cry, boys crying showed weakness.
Whenever he'd catch himself crying he'd beat himself to stop him from crying, and beat himself til he was numb. He beat himself to punish himself for feeling.

 

8 years later..

 

San, was now 14 years old, scurrying down the busy streets, he was constantly looking behind him - watching if anyone was following him. It was daytime and he was running through the city wearing dark clothing despite the spring weather, he'd ran off from school and was making his way to a grocery store.

He walked inside the grocery store and walked past the worker who greeted him - he barely acknowledged her, only giving her a glance in response.

He opened his jacket, pretending as if he was hot and was trying to cool off, before reaching a hand inside the cooler and pulling out a pepsi can and shoving it inside his jacket.
He made his way over to the alcohol and shoved some expensive alcohol into his bag, and then making his way out of the grocery store, giving a small nod and smile to the counter to not raise any suspicion, he was used to doing this. He'd done this several times before.

He rounded a corner when he felt a hand grab him and pull him into an alleyway and then being shoved into a wall, he grunted and looked up to see a man a few years older than him, not much but it was clear he was an adult.

" What the fuck is your problem??" He spoke, about to throw hands.

" Now now, you don't need to get angry." The man spoke.

San took notice of his appearance, a brown mullet with rings on his fingers.
He was about the same height as him, and San being 15 years old was surprised, this man would definitely be shorter than him in a few years.

San crossed his arms and stared at the man.

" Who are you?" He asked after looking him up and down a few seconds.

The man stared at San for a few seconds before looking towards the street beside them.

" I'm Hongjoong, Kim Hongjoong." He replied before turning back to look at San.

San stared in silence at Hongjoong.

" Okay.. I'm Sa-"
" We know."

"... We? " San asked, frowning.

And as if it was planned, a car pulled up into the alleyway, a Silver Mercedes Benz E63 AMG, and 2 guys stepped out with 2 girls, San stared at them in disbelief, who are these people?

" We've been watching you for a while, San."
Hongjoong said and eyed San up and down, San looked at the people.

The guy closing the driver's-side door looked to be about Hongjoong's age, maybe a year older and definitely taller, he had very black hair and an undercut.

" What.. why?? who the hell are you guys??" San asked, frustrated that he wasn't being given the answers to what he wanted to know.

" You're wasting good potential robbing from grocery stores you know." One of the girls spoke, she had long purple hair and wore a black blazer and black suit pants, underneath her blazer was a white button-up shirt, well she's clearly not hetero.

" Good potential??" San asked again.

" Alright kid, let's just cut to the chase; You're joining Shax on an important heist in 2 weeks. You'll begin your training tomorrow." Hongjoong spoke, sighing a bit.

" You don't have a say in the matter, either you join or we kill you."
The purple haired woman chuckled.

San rolled his eyes. Great...

~

" That's how San joined Shax?" Wooyoung asked Mingi.

" Not exactly, he joined them on that one heist, but Hongjoong felt like he couldn't let San go, he felt like San was a danger to himself. Especially after seeing all his self-harm scars and witnessing one of his schizophrenia episodes." Mingi answered and sighed a bit.

" One night, he happend to be going through a rough episode. His mother had heard his groans, whimpers and grunts and had entered the room.. and San in a fit of rage.. stabbed her to death with the pocket knife he had hidden in his room.." Mingi continued.

~

" So you killed your own mother?" Hongjoong asked, he was silent and just looking at San.

San was drunk, they'd been drinking at a bar.

" Yes, but the b-bitch deserved it." San said, he was hiccuping a little, holding the shotglass in his hand.

" San-" Hongjoong began, but San was talking to himself, completely ignoring what Hongjoong was saying. Hongjoong would be lying if he said that San didn't scare him with how nonchalant he was being about everything considering the circumstances.
Hongjoong also thought that he could barely see some blood splatter on San's clothing but he tried not looking at it too much.

" I actually killed my own fucking mother. Can you believe that?" San said while chuckling.

" No, I can't." Hongjoong spoke, staring at San in disbelief, he turned away from San and stared out into space and sighed.
This kid is not okay, he's barely 15 years old and he's laughing about how he just killed his own mother.
That's when Hongjoong heard a sniffle from San, he turned to the boy and saw that San was crying.

" Both of my parents are dead and I'm only 15 years old." San said while chuckling, he was crying while smiling... it broke Hongjoong's heart.

~

" Hongjoong knew that San was a danger to himself, especially in the state he was in. So he decided to bring San into the gang.
The gang treated San like he was their little brother, they trained him to become one of them and San began trusting Hongjoong and relying on him for when he needed it." Mingi spoke. Wooyoung stared at Mingi in utter disbelief.

" How do you know all of this? I tried getting him to talk to me but San said he never opens up to people because he learnt it the hard way!" Wooyoung said and stared at Mingi, he was growing suspicious, was Mingi really telling the truth or not?

Mingi sighed deeply, he felt a gut-wrenching feeling in his chest at Wooyoung's question.

" I'm the reason he doesn't open up anymore."

Wooyoung was silent, he let the words sink into his chest gradually,
What did Mingi do to break San's trust?
Why did Mingi do what he did?

" What did you do?" Wooyoung asked Mingi a bit hesitant but serious, at this point he'd completely forgotten to write whatever Mingi said down on his notebook, and all his attention was on the man infront of him who's tearing up.

 

3 years ago..

 

" You fucking prick!"

" I'll fucking kill you!!"

" You're dead to me!!!"

Mingi heard ringing through his head as he laid in the Nursery-room - being tended by the Shax's doctor, he'd been shot in his stomach, shoulder blade and foot while fighting with San.

The room looked like a hospital, only difference being the smell of cigarettes floating in the air and the fella who was treating Mingi being a gangster.

" Why are you here in the first place Mingi?" The nurse asked Mingi, Mingi sighed and groaned a little in pain as the doctor cared for his bullet-wounds.

" I stabbed someone in the back for some fucking drugs and money." Mingi replied, regret and dread was evident in his tone.

" Who did you piss off this time?" The nurse asked, receiving a small grumble from Mingi.

" Choi San.." Mingi replied quietly after grumbling to himself about the situation and the circumstances, the nurse stopped cleaning the wound on his shoulder and looked at Mingi with a serious expression.

" Dude, you're dead once he finds you."

" You don't think I know that?" Mingi replied, he shifted a little on the bed but instantly regretted even moving an inch because an eletrical bolt of pain shot through his body making him moan out in pain.

 

'' San though, refuses to let someone know his own weakness.

He opened up once to someone he thought he could trust, he won't ever make that mistake again. He learnt the hard way that you shouldn't open up too easily if ever. '' - chapter 008

 

Mingi recalled the moments where San was staring down at him with a wild and dangerous look in his eyes.
A dark, sadistic, glint in San's eyes as Mingi was squirming on the ground and hugging his stomach from being kicked straight in the gut.

" S..Sa-" Mingi tried speaking out but San kicked him in his stomach again making Mingi gag and groan while choking on his words.

" You're a fucking fraud, I fucking trusted you with my past and you go out telling it to the enemies for fucking drugs and money??!!." San shouted at Mingi and kicked him in the face with his foot, Mingi felt a metallic taste emerge in his mouth.

" I could just fucking snap your head and kill you right here right now." San said, glaring at Mingi with the same cold look in his eyes.

" You know what I woke up to today in my house?! Some fucking pruny Diablos raiders in my home looking to kill me." San growled, he tightened the ropes already tied around Mingi's wrists who were behind his back, he made sure to tighten it enough for Mingi's blood circulation to stop, he wanted the latter to suffer.

" That's not all, remember my sister? The ONLY family I have left?!!!" San asked, walking over to the table he had various weapons and tools.

No response came from Mingi as San picked up a bat, San turned his head to look at Mingi who was laying on the ground barely responsive from the beating and the shot-wounds, San shook his head in disbelief before walking over to Mingi and grabbing his chin aggressively to make the man look up at him.

" Do you?!" San repeated himself, some spit flying in Mingi's face. Mingi couldn't care less about the spit though because he could actually feel his stomach turning in dread of what was coming.

Mingi nodded, he knew exactly which sister San was talking about.

It was his baby sister that San was talking about, the one who was only 4 months old before San killed his own mother, San had sworn to keep her out of anything related to gangs and outlaws as much as possible.

" Bangchan is holding her fucking captive somewhere and is demanding me 700M ₩ in exchange for my sister, my sister who is EIGHT FUCKING YEARS OLD." San screamed the last 4 words in Mingi's face. Mingi looked down in shame, he regretted every single decision he'd made up until that point.

" S-San, b-believe me... i-if I c-could t-turn back t-time-"

" But you can't, can you?" San interrupted, gripping his hair tightly making Mingi wince and moan in pain.

" If captain doesn't kick you out of Shax I will do it myself." San threatened.

Mingi knew he would be without a doubt kicked out of Shax, if not killed by San alone.
The thing with San; he'd never kill you in an instant.
No.. he'd torture you, until you're on the brink of death, until you're barely responsive, before he'd let you go. And once he's let you go - he'll follow you everywhere, he will make sure you know that he's watching you - so that you always live in fear and despair.

Mingi knows that survivors of San's abuse has said that it's better to just die, and several of them has committed suicide, because of how severe and brutal it is.

And if Mingi could move, he'd for sure beat himself up for even thinking of stabbing San in the back like that, but he'd tied up and is in unbearable pain and is facing San's wrath, so he's gonna sit and endure it, and then he'll punish himself for it.

Wooyoung sat in shock, and I'm not exaggerating when I say his jaw was on the floor.

San has a baby sister???

What did San do to him?
Also what about his older brothers??

One thing Wooyoung knew for certain though, is that the next time he and Changbin is looking through the 'Missing People files' he's gonna take an extra good look.

The latter plopped down beside him on the kitchen stools while swinging his feet back and forth, lightly but involuntarily bumping it against the kitchen counter every once in a while.

" No!! It's that damn odor that keeps returning, it smells like an actual rotting animal." Changbin defended himself, only to get Wooyoung choking on his cereal when reminded that Changbin wasn't far from right.

He'd have to somehow convince the maintance and cleaning ladies to look into the odor more instead of just ignoring it because the odor has been here for quite some time and he doesn't want to waste any more time and letting San and his buddies fill up the station with even more decomposing bodies.

A dead and supposedly murdered body was found on the ground that seemed to be decomposing.

The smell was horrid and disgusting, Wooyoung had already thrown up once from the smell and he wouldn't be surprised if he threw up once more - but the latter decided it'd be best to man up as this was his job and his way of income, even though he'd prefer if he wouldn't have to stare at dead corpses for a change.

" It seems like the tire tracks and skid marks are from some bikes, did you find any fingerprints inside the building?" Wooyoung asked, referring to the abandoned building not far away.

Wooyoung's stomach turned a bit and he had to physically stop himself from puking again at that.

" Well ain't we lucky?" Wooyoung said sarcastically and sighed.

01:52

 

Rain was pouring, soaking the streets and the few people walking around, including a certain someone dressed in a black leather jacket, a white tee and jeans.

He was leaning against a brickwall and watching his nearby parked matte black Kawasaki Ninja ZX-10r with the helmet resting on the seat. He was smoking a cigarette and exhaled the smoke into the air.
From where he stood he luckily didn't get any rain on him, not that he really cared if he did or not, he enjoyed the feeling of being soaking wet by the rain, unlike most people who would mostly only complain and sulk about the bad weather - he on the other hand preferred the rain and he didn't look at it as bad weather.

His thoughts were running wild.

 

Jung Wooyoung you annoying piece of shit.

You guessed it, it was the infamous Choi San we're talking about, and he was having complicated thoughts about a certain individual.

San looked down to his arms, the bandages that Wooyoung used on him.

Why?
Why why why why why??

San kicked a pebble with his foot, groaning.
He took a drag from the cigarette and let the smoke fill his lungs before exhaling the smoke out.

He never cared.

He heard footsteps stopping to the side of him, a boot scraping against the asfalt, he didn't even have to turn to know that it was Hongjoong.

" I wanna know what is up with you. " He asked, he leaned against the brickwall beside San and pulled out a cigar and lit it with his lighter, his hair was dripping wet and he ran a hand through it.

" Nothing. " He replied simply without looking at him, he was a very secretive person and didn't scare his secrets, not after what happened with Mingi.
He swore up and beyond not to open up again.

" You know that's not true, you keep zoning out and you're always deep in your thoughts. " Hongjoong replies and looks at San, his eyes twinkled a little when looking at the younger.

million times more. " San replied and took a drag from his cigarette.

He wished San could learn to accept that people care about him.
He wished San wouldn't blame everything on himself.

Hongjoong looked towards San, his expression stayed the same, blank but slightly worried.
Hongjoong wasn't a man who cared much about anyone, the only people he cared about was the people close to him.

and turned to look at the older.

let out a big yawn as right now the only source for his will to live is that his bed is waiting for him at home.

" I said shut up! " Wooyoung replied, a bit more sternly now.

Just then a metal clinging sound sounded out again, a louder one now, and then a voice could be barely heard.

" Don't take so long. " Echoed out from the vents.

Wooyoung and Changbin slowly turned their heads to look at eachother, Changbin tiptoed over to Wooyoung. " Was that you? " He asked.

Wooyoung just gave him an unamused look and tiptoed over to the vent that the sounds were coming from.

He put his ear up to the vents and covered his other ear to listen, he heard the distant sound of water running.
The sewers........

Wooyoung nearly threw up when remembering that the most likely explanation for the metal clinging sound and the person saying 'don't take so long' would be Shax dumping more corpses.....

" It's coming from the vent leading down to the sewers.. " Wooyoung whispered to Changbin.

" Well what do we do? " Changbin asked.

" We're police officers aren't we? " Wooyoung asked Changbin.

" Yes..? Your point? "

" So we go down and check it out! " Wooyoung said, quickly walking past Changbin and to their cubicle to grab his jacket.

" Are you trying to get us killed?! "

" It's our duty Changbin, it comes with it's risks. " Wooyoung replied, shoving his pistol into his gun holster that hung on his belt and looked up at him.

" Come on. " Wooyoung said, patting Changbin on the back as he made his way to the exit of the building that led outside to the back where the entrance to the sewers were.

" Oh fuck you Jung Wooyoung. " Changbin said as he followed him.

 

" You're taking so goddamn long! " One of the men complained, he was leaning against the Black SUV and watched the other that was currently throwing corpses into the sewers through the manhole cover.

The man leaning against the SUV had blonde hair in a tiny small ponytail, he wore a black cargo vest and a white crop top underneath, black cargo pants finishing with some black boots.

The other had brown middle split curly hair, he had a white button-up that had the top 3 buttons unbuttoned and black leather pants with black platform boots.

" I'm not taking long! It's just you who's the impatient one here Hyunjin. " The other replied.

The unnamed one wore black latex gloves that reached his elbows, he dragged a bag full of cut off limbs to the manhole cover and dumped them down, the bag hit the metal ladder and made a loud sound.

" Han could you be anymore louder?! " Hyunjin asked.

The supposed Han stood up straight and looked at Hyunjin with a slight annoyed look on his face.

" Do you want me to be louder? " Han asked genuinely and crossed his arms, picture it like how a diva would.

" That was sarcasm you nutsack!! " Hyunjin said, the said man bent down, picked up a big rock and threw it in Han's direction. Han ducked and the rock flew down into the sewers through the hole, hitting the ladder and making an even louder sound.

The two glared at eachother and started blaming eachother for making loud noises.

" Stop arguing and just get the job done. " A voice called out, the two of them turned their heads and saw 2 men standing to the side.

The owner of the voice had a brown mullet, he looked like the oldest of the 4. He wore a long brown opened fur coat and a simple black tshirt and black jeans underneath with black suit shoes.

The other had Black short hair, his bangs long and covering his eyes, he had a leather jacket over a white tee and jeans with some black boots.

The 2 were also known as Hongjoong and San.

Hongjoong had a cigar hung loosely between his lips and San stood beside them and parked his Kawasaki on the side of the road.

" Yes boss. " Hyunjin and Han said in synch, Hyunjin handed Han a second bag of limbs which was then thrown into the sewer by the ladder.

San stood off the side and didn't say anything, he was more of an observer, he wore a blank expression as he pulled out a cigarette and put it in between his lips. He pulled out a lighter and lit the cigarette, smoke filling his lungs and blowing out into the air.
He exhaled the smoke deeply, thinking about the conversation he'd just had with Hongjoong not many minutes ago.

Why the hell did I ask him that? Rang out in his head, he was deep in thought, he looked down to his arms yet again, reminded by a certain individual yet again.

He's an idiot, a big piece of shit for treating me like that.
You may be wondering why the hell San is mad at Wooyoung for treating him nicely?

Well.. San doesn't believe in doing 'good deeds' or anything of that sort.
He feels like now he owes something to Wooyoung, and he hates that feeling so much, it's like a loud voice whispering in his ear, a constant reminder.

He already owes a lot to his uncle, atleast that's what he tells himself. His uncle did nothing but remind him of how he was disgusting and how it was his fault for his father's death, and San genuinely believed it, and now he owed something back to his uncle and his father big time.

 

Wooyoung and Changbin tiptoed out of the big room with all the cubicles and out into the big hallways, they passed the multiple questioning rooms and such, they quietly made their way to the exit doors that led out into the back.

They were being as quiet as possible, and heard voices on the other side.

" Stop arguing and just get the job done. "

" Yes boss. "

Wooyoung and Changbin turned to look at eachother.

" This is such a bad plan. " Changbin whisper-shouted to Wooyoung, who just gave him a small glare in response.

" I never said it was a good plan. " Wooyoung whispered back.

It's good that they're being self-aware.

Wooyoung reached for the handle of the door after some seconds with building up the courage to open it quietly, as quietly as possible.
It opened a crack, and revealed the 4 men outside.

Changbin found himself observing their appearance, the smell coming from the open manhole cover was disgusting and overwhelming and Changbin was struggling with not throwing up at the moment, he didn't understand how the men weren't on the ground gagging already.

Wooyoung had immediately recognized one of them as San because of his looks and tall build.
He shifted his gaze from San for a second to look at Changbin with a horrified look on his face.

Wooyoung then proceeded to turn back to look at San only to find that the said man was already staring straight at them.

The one who he almost hooked up with.

The one who bandaged him.

The one who he hated.

Wooyoung had trouble getting words out as he and San locked eyes with eachother, it seemed like San was the only one out of the 3 to notice Wooyoung and Changbin.

A small but noticable smirk grew on San's face before he flashed a wink at Wooyoung.

San turned back to Hongjoong, Hyunjin and Han and watched them dump the last of the corpses and bags of limbs in the sewer.

Wooyoung and Changbin shared a look with eachother.

And Changbin, being the big... big... BIG idiot he is spoke quietly to Wooyoung:

" Do you think they saw us? "

In a split second, all eyes were on Wooyoung and Changbin.

Wooyoung figured that since the men already had seen them, why hide?

" Drop the weapon. " Wooyoung spoke, glaring straight at Hongjoong who held a revolver with a stern look on his face, Wooyoung however seemed rather unamused.

" No. " Hongjoong replied, a cocky smirk on his face.

San just watched from the side, his jaw clenched shut tightly. His muscles were clenching and unclenching.

" You realize who you're talking to.. right? " Hyunjin asked with an annoying grin on his face, Han had a similar grin plastered on his face aswell.
It was clear that the 2 of them were that one annoying but iconic duo based on their chemistry.

San spat out his cigarette and stomped on it, crushing it.

" Listen, officers. We're either gonna do this the easy way or the hard way. It's your pick but if I were you, I'd pick the easy way. " Hongjoong spoke and coldly chuckled a little.

Wooyoung was clenched his fist, he contemplated lowering the pistol but didn't and still had it pointed straight at Hongjoong.
Hyunjin and Han watched, their gaze switching back and forth between Wooyoung and their boss.

" Well, you put this on yourself." Hongjoong said before doing a signal to Hyunjin and Han, who both pulled up knives and was about to lunge at Wooyoung and Changbin when San suddenly grabbed the back of both Hyunjin's and Han's collar making them halt and gag from the sudden pressure around their necks.

" Don't. " San said sternly, glaring straight at the both of them.

Hongjoong stared at San with his eyes narrowed. San looked at Hongjoong and just sighed and shook his head, his hair was soaking wet and he had to dry his eyes because water was dripping down his forehead and into his eyes.

Wooyoung stared at San with a surprised expression on his face. He did not expect that from San, his eyebrows furrowed.

Hyunjin, although slightly reluctant he entered the driver's seat, Han did aswell and went to sit down in the middle seat.

Hongjoong turned to look at San as he heard the doors of the SUV shutting, he approached him.
" Why did you- " He began, only to be cut off by San.

" Don't, I'll talk to you later. " San spoke, glaring at Hongjoong.
San's intense and fierce eyes were enough to make the infamous Hongjoong gulp and nod before walking to the SUV and sitting down in the right seat.

Shutting the door, the SUV rolled out of the parking lot and sped off. Wooyoung stared at San with narrowed eyes as San was walking over to his motorcycle, helmet in hand.

" What? " San asked, he could feel Wooyoung's eyes on him.

It was still pouring rain outside, and by now Changbin had returned to the inside of the station, where it was warm and comfortable, unlike the weather outside at the moment. Wooyoung hadn't noticed this as he was staring straight at San with multiple questions rising in his head.

" Why did you do that? " Wooyoung asked, he walked over to San who in response to Wooyoung's question had paused what he was doing paused what he was doing.

San turned to look at Wooyoung, with closed eyes and clenched fists.

" Don't bother asking. " San replied.

Wooyoung just stared at him in silence. San looked away from Wooyoung and sighed deeply, an awkward silence fell between them excluding the pouring rain and distant thunder.

" Just go home Wooyoung. " San spoke, not looking at Wooyoung.

" I owe you. " Wooyoung spoke.

" No, you don't. "

" Yes I do, you didn't have to do that. "

" You didn't have to take care of me. " San replied and glared at him, Wooyoung stayed silent at that.

San was somewhat true.

" I just did what anyone would do. " Wooyoung muttered after some seconds and looked away.

San gripped Wooyoung's chin and turned his head to look at him. " Never do something like that again, Do you understand? " San said sternly and when Wooyoung didn't reply San leaned in even closer.

" I said.. do you understand? " He repeated himself.

" Yes.. " Wooyoung said, looking in San's eyes.
San nodded to himself.

" Good boy. "

San said with a small smirk on his face, he watched Wooyoung in silence for a few seconds before letting go of the boy's chin.

He walked away from Wooyoung and sat down on his Black Kawasaki, he put on his matching helmet and turned to look at Wooyoung.
He turned on the ignition and revved the bike, revelling in the way Wooyoung's eyes lit up as the latter watched.
San rode off into the night without another word said to Wooyoung, and the said man just stood there silently, thinking about the last 5 minutes of happenings.

What the hell just happened?

" I don't think us two alone could conquer 4 men, they outnumbered us and let's face it; they're more likely to overpower us considering they fight eachother daily. "
Changbin said, stating more or less the obvious.

" I guess you're right.. " Wooyoung replied.

 

The rain had slowed down, you could barely call it rain. The thunder was still loud as the large cloud containing the thunder and lightning was passing by directly above Seoul.

San was up on the roof of the Shax building, he smoked a cigarette, his feet dangling off of the building.

He pulled up his phone, it read 4:41 am, November 8th.

November 8th.. his uncle's birthday.

He sighed deeply, the date November 8th was a constant reminder of the consistent abuse and torture that he had to endure at a ridiculously young age.

The abuse from his uncle never stopped, not up until the night he killed his own mother.
He'd cut off all contact with his uncle, and the only family he wished to be in contact with was his little sister, though he didn't even know if she was still alive this day, and if she was she would be 11 years old.

Oh how San missed her bright smile.

He broke out of his thoughts when someone coughed a little to get his attention, San knew who it was alone by his soft breathing and his cough.

" It's November 8th.. " Hongjoong said.

Hongjoong went and sat down beside San, also dangling his tiny feet off the rooftop and looking out at Seoul.

San didn't reply, he just took a drag of his cigarette and blew the smoke out into the air, the cigarette rested between his pointer and middle finger.

Hongjoong turned to look at San, he knew that the latter was probably not doing that great considering the circumstances. San was far from one to speak up about whenever he struggled. Hongjoong had to learn on his own how to know when San was under the weather, through the hard way.

What do I mean by the hard way?
He had to lose San 2 times for him to finally start understanding the signs and when San would be close to his mental breakdowns.

He can still recall that one time, when San was only fucking 16 years old. It was the first time he'd witness San almost kill himself.

 

San had relapsed and was currently sitting in the shower, on the bathroom tiles with his back leaning back against the wall.

He wore a loose thin black sweater, the sleeves were scrunched up to his elbows and his arms were cut up.. bad.
In his hand he held a blade loosely as he stared out at the wall infront of him.
He was soaked in the water running from the showerhead, the water mixing with his blood made it look like a blood bath.

And If it wasn't for his brain dampening the pain with adrenaline, he would be screaming in pain...

He should be screaming in pain.

San didn't even react when Hongjoong burst through the door. Hongjoong picked him up and made him stand on his feet. San had completely zoned out and his eyes were dull. He looked lifeless.

Actual dead corpses couldn't even be compared to him right now.

" What the fuck did you do??? " Hongjoong shouted.

At 21 years old, Hongjoong had never seen someone abuse their own arms as much as San had done.
San looked to be really gone. If Hongjoong hadn't gotten there sooner or if he didn't get help San would most likely bleed to death.
But Hongjoong won't let that happen, not now, not tomorrow or ever.

Hongjoong slapped his face, trying to get him to respond but San stayed unresponsive, and it wasn't until he'd gotten Seonghwa, Shax's nurse to help, that he woke up.

Seonghwa got to work with stitching Seonghwa's many cuts and wounds on his arms. San stayed still and watched Seonghwa and how his slender fingers handled the needle. Anyone sane in their minds would turn away, but San? He watched.

Hongjoong remembered the serious and deep conversation he had with San afterwards.

" Why? " Hongjoong asked, he was standing infront of San in the office with a stern look on his face.

San didn't reply and looked away, though he's anything but sane and definitely grew up too early, he too can act like a stubborn teenager, and now was anything but different.

" You might aswell say it San because I'm not gonna stop asking until you- " Hongjoong spoke, before San decided to cut him off.

" I walked in on my uncle hitting my little sister. "
San spoke and turned to look away from Hongjoong, who was frowning in response to what San said.

If you knew about his little sister, you'd know that she was his everything, his world, his universe, and he'd protect her with all he got.
He blamed himself for anything and everything that happened to her, and he swore up and down to protect her but he failed to do exactly that.

" Then what? " Hongjoong asked.

And San just shrugged in reply.

" I blacked out from my anger. " He replied, a cold shiver went down his spine from how sinister, his voice sounded.

Hongjoong later found out that what San did, was approach his uncle with a knife he held and stab him before pulling his sister away and out.

 

" You're crying, Boss. " San's chilly and nonchalant voice sounded out, breaking Hongjoong from his deep thoughts.

Hongjoong cleared his throat and wiped his tears off his cheeks, it never really hit him how much he's grown to San. He almost looked at him as if San was his own son, and it hurt him to see San like this.

" Sorry. " Hongjoong spoke while clearing his voice a bit, he felt a little embarrassed for being caught crying like that.

He sighed and rubbed the back of his neck. San flicked the ashes on the cigarette and watched it fall down the edge of the building, he brought the cigarette back to his lips and inhaled the smoke.

" You remember that we're bombing 3 Diablos warehouses tomorrow, right? " Hongjoong said, trying to change the topic yet also reminding him.

" Yes.. " San grumbled and rolled his eyes.

" Try not to kill Mingi if you see him. " Hongjoong said with a slight stern voice but also slightly worried because he knew what San was capable of doing and he wouldn't be surprised if San actually killed Mingi.

" I can't make a promise if it's not guaranteed if I can hold it or not, you know I hate promises. "
San says and glares at Hongjoong, who just sighs and nods - figuring there's no use in trying to argue.

 

" You okay? " Wooyoung asked Changbin, who currently had his head buried deep in a trashbin that he was holding and was gagging, coughing and spitting - trying to not throw up for the 3rd time in 2 hours. He gave a weak thumbs up that almost looked comical.

It was currently 11am, and they were watching crime scene workers pull out body bags.. after body bags.. after body bags.. after body bags of victims from the sewers down below.

Wooyoung even thought he heard one of the crime workers mutter "I deserve a raise for this."

He didn't blame them though, this was disgusting and he'd much rather stay at home under a blanket rather than standing outside and volunteering with cleaning up and maintaining the sewers.

Soobin and Yunho were standing off to the side and speaking with one of the team leaders of the crime workers, discussing how many the bags are and such.

" How many are we talking? " Yunho asked with crossed arms and jaw clenched.

Wooyoung couldn't lie, whenever Yunho put on his serious face and got in his chief role it got his heart running and blood pumping.

Was it unprofessional considering how he's one of the bosses? Probably.
Did he care though? Not really.

" We've pulled out 13 bags so far, it's estimated that there are approximately 25 left. " The team leader spoke, a slight chill running up his spine.

" What about bodies, how many do you suppose there is? " Soobin spoke, with narrowed eyes.

The team leader turned to look towards the manhole cover, sighing a bit.
He heard many news reporters and writers yell out and screaming, asking for any crime workers or officers to be interviewed.
They were being obnoxious, disrespectful and not to mention very loud.

He turned to look back at Soobin, frowning a little.
" We've counted so far 11 bodies, as 2 of the bags contain body parts and remnants of organs, but we could be talking 50+. " He spoke and sighed, Wooyoung unfortunately overheard this and felt his heart churn with hatred.

While patting Changbin's back as Changbin finally gave in and started throwing up, he started thinking of possible ways to prevent this from happening, such as talking with San? Bribing him into stopping whatever they're doing?
I mean, they clearly want something when Shax are killing and murdering this amount of people.

" Wooyoung? "

Wooyoung's thoughts were quickly cut short when Yeosang's voice called out, he turned to look at the older to see Yeonjun also standing beside him.

Stupid idea to do this, Wooyoung thought.

I don't get paid enough to do this, Wooyoung thought.

Why am I even doing this? Wooyoung thought.

I hate phone calls to death. Wooyoung thought.

Wooyoung leaned against the kitchen counter back in his home with his phone up to his ear, waiting for the person he was calling to pick up.
He looked over to the clock on the wall, 04:54 pm..
The phone rang a couple of times before someone picked up, the sound of someone covering the mic for some seconds sounded out before..

" Hello? " Mingi's voice echoed from the speakers of Wooyoung's phone.

" Hey, it's me Wooyoung. " Wooyoung spoke, he bit the nail on his thumb.

" Oh hi Wooyoung! How you been? " Mingi asked, Wooyoung could hear the smile through Mingi's voice, if you didn't know Mingi then you'd never guess he was a gangster.

Everything about Mingi is soft, he's like a walking teddy bear.
He's a hopeless romantic, a gummy smile so cute that Wooyoung wants to squeeze him to death, loves skin-ship and adores puppies and kittens. If anything Wooyoung would have guessed that Mingi would have worked at a flower shop or a cafè because he would just seem like one to do exactly that, but no. He's a gangster.

" I've been.. better I guess. Work is stressful. " Wooyoung said and groaned into the phone.

" I'd guess so, I saw it on the news... how many bodies showed up? " Mingi asked after some time, Wooyoung sensed that Mingi's smile disappeared on the other line.

" In the end it came up as 31 bodies, 38 bags in total, meaning that 6 of them were filled with body parts and... organs. " Wooyoung responded, grimacing a bit at the last comment.

The other line was silent for some time, as if Mingi wasn't even in the room.

" Mingi? You still there? " Wooyoung asked.

" Oh, yeah sorry.. I got just.. a bit distracted. " Mingi replied, his voice almost sounding like he was in a whole another world.

" It's fine.. I was wondering about something.. " Wooyoung said, he did not know how Mingi would reply to the next comment but figured that he should just risk it all as he had nothing to lose.

" What? "

Wooyoung took a deep breath.

" Do you know where I could find.. San? " He asked.

The line went silent, Wooyoung was afraid that Mingi would hang up but he never did.
" Yes. " Mingi replied after sometime, his voice suddenly grown a little darker and somber.

" .... Well where? Could you perhaps maybe tell me? " Wooyoung asked, chewing his thumb nail so much he started to bleed.

He put the phone down on the counter to find a bandaid, he had it on speaker mode so that he could still listen to Mingi speaking.

" One of his many hide outs, at the Yongsan Family Park, usually somewhere between night or sunset. "

" May I ask why though? " Mingi asked shortly after.

" I'm gonna give San a piece of my thoughts, he and Shax have no reason to do the things they did. " Wooyoung spoke, re-fastening the bandaid a second time after failing once.

" Don't play the main character here Wooyoung, remember who you're potentially messing with here. " Mingi replied, chuckling a little in disbelief.

" I'll be fine, I'll have my pistol with me.. " Wooyoung said and sighed a little, slumping backwards until his back rested against the kitchen counter behind him as he stared at the phone facing upwards on the island infront of him.

" Just don't piss him off too much, he's worse than satan once he gets mad. " Mingi said, in a little stern voice. Mingi in reality wanted Wooyoung to stay the fuck away from San but he wouldn't tell Wooyoung that as he'd learned by now that the latter was as stubborn as an 8 year old.

And with that, they hung up and said there goodbyes, Wooyoung sighed and ran a hand through his hair. He had to get this done with. He'll give him a piece of his mind and also try to convince San to stop bringing the station into their shenanigans - it is very risky, it's not a thing he needs to do, this is purely out of pure will. Why though?

He feels like he's almost drawn into the other man. It's like he's almost.. excited to meet and talk to him again.

... Why?

The 3rd spot, a baseball court passes by, and Wooyoung was starting to yawn a bit and eventually start to grow tired of walking.
He checks the clock and sees 00:47, silently groaning and cursing to himself.

After some time.. the 4th spot, a stone bridge comes into view. He thought he could smell cigarette smoke.

He slowly walked over to the side of the bridge, peeking around the corner, he couldn't see anyone but he could smell the cigarette smoke.

A twig snapped from behind him and in a matter of seconds he was thrown into the stone wall behind him, his breath knocked out of his lungs. He opened his eyes to see a pair of fierce and cold eyes staring back at Wooyoung and a raised fist in the air about to punch him.

" Hey hey hey hey!!! San it's me, I don't want any harm! " Wooyoung said, putting his hands up in surrender, San had a threatening grip on Wooyoung's sweater.

Wooyoung wore clothes suggesting that he was off duty, he wore a grey sweater and baggy jeans, hoping it wouldn't set off the other.

San glared at him, still not letting go of Wooyoung.

" What do you want? Why the hell are you looking for me? " San growled, pushing Wooyoung more into the stones behind him making the latter bit back a moan in pain from the stones digging into his spine.
" I have a bone to pick with you. " Wooyoung spoke, with his eyes narrowed.

San glared at him before shoving him further into the wall. " Oh yeah? " He said with that annoying smirk, his cold eyes dug into Wooyoung.

" How about you stop murdering all the people you do!!? And stop bringing the station in it!!
You have no idea how many times I saw someone throw up when we cleaned the sewers. "
Wooyoung complained, trying to make San let go of his sweater.

" I couldn't care less. " San replied simply, his fist still raised in the air.

" God you're so insufferable!! Don't you have some fucking feelings?!!??" Wooyoung spoke in a groan, and it hurt San, even though the latter didn't know exactly why it would.¨

 

Why did Wooyoung's words hurt?

 

San tightened his grip on Wooyoung's shirt, veins popping out on his forearm and upper hand.

" I don't care about you and your little officers. What I do is none of your business, this is a result of your own actions sweetheart. " San said and let go of Wooyoung, taking a drag of his cigarette and blowing the smoke out in Wooyoung's face.

It was dark out and Wooyoung could barely see his face but it looked like he was getting a black eye, his lower lip was also split open not far away from where his lip was pierced.

" Don't sweetheart me. " Wooyoung said, dusting dust and dirt off his clothes while glaring at San.

San leaned down to the younger, his hands on his thighs with the signature smug smirk.

" Sweetheart. " He repeated while grinning.

Wooyoung rolled his eyes though his cheek felt warmer, he convinced himself it was the smoke from San's cigarette but that didn't make sense, he didn't care though because he wanted everything to be platonic here.
Nothing romantic between the two, nuh-uh. He hates San to death.... probably.. hopefully.

 

" Don't you guys have your own place to bury the bodies? " Wooyoung asked after a few seconds, trying to change the topic as the air was getting a little awkward now.

" Yes, under your station. "

" No but like.. at your hide-out or something. "

" Nope, your station has been multiple of our victims' cemetery for many years. " San replied.
" I never understood why but it's something Hongjoong insisted. " San continued after some seconds of silence.

San sat down on a bench under the bridge and stared out at the big river infront of them, the only sounds other than their breathing being the water flowing and the slight rustle of leaves and branches from the mild wind, Wooyoung stared at San as San just sat there in silence.

He invited himself over and sat down beside San, he tried not thinking about how... weird it was that he kept ending up with San.

He was deep in thought, rubbing his elbow as he'd scraped it on the stones from when San had pinned him there, he was pretty sure he'd gotten a scratch-mark there.

" I have a compromise. " Wooyoung began, staring at the river infront of them.
San didn't turn to look at him, he just continued smoking his cigarette in silence for a little while before eventually answering with " What? "
His head was turned away from Wooyoung as San looked around the coast, looking for anyone who perhaps might be following him.. or now them.

" How about.. you help me solve cases from Shax, victims. Help bringing closure to families so that you can do something good for once and in return, I help find your little sister. "
Wooyoung spoke, turning to look at San.

San paused, his fists and jaw clenched.

He slowly turned his head to look at Wooyoung, a dark fire in his eyes and him licking his lips.

" I never told you that I have a little sister. "

I'm so fucked, I'm so fucked, I'm so fucked, I'm so fucked, I'm so fucking fucked.

Wooyoung felt his blood run cold, and a chill run up his spine.

" I, uh.. w-well, y-you see.. " Wooyoung stuttered, immediately regretting showing up here to talk to San.

" How do you know I have a little sister?" San asked in a dark whisper, glaring straight into Wooyoung's eyes, Wooyoung couldn't reply even if he wanted to. His mouth was as dry as a fucking desert and it felt like he was gonna die if he didn't get any water in the next few hours.

Wooyoung was looking away from San, feeling very lightheaded yet heavy-headed, all at once.
He didn't dare look at the older, afraid to meet his destined death.

So this is how I die. Amazing. Atleast he's hot.
Wooyoung thought to himself, a little unserious but figured that he's allowed to be unserious when San is glaring at him as if he was a ghost.

San gripped Wooyoung's chin to turn his head to look at him.

" Answer the fucking question Wooyoung." He growled softly, his voice as cold as ice. His eyes resembled those of satan, or worse, and the fact that his voice is as quiet as it is doesn't make it any fucking better.

" I d-don't.." Wooyoung muttered, the rest of his words died in his throat which left a tense silence. San clenched his fists together in frustration, taking a deep breath at the same time.

" How do you know.. about my little sister...? " San asked in a quiet tone, inching closer to Wooyoung while tilting his head to the side. Wooyoung backed away from San.

" Answer the question Wooyoung. " San growled, and when Wooyoung didn't answer San had had enough. " Fine, I'll do it the hard way then. " San spoke, before gripping Wooyoung's wrists. " Hey!! Let me go!" He yells. " No. " San spoke, dragging him along the park, Wooyoung tried pulling away from San but to no avail.

He starts to drag Wooyoung along with him to his car. Wooyoung refuses and squirms like how a child would do when being dragged out of the candy-store.

San shoves Wooyoung into the backseat, crawling in after him and shutting the cardoor. Wooyoung tried sitting up but was pushed down by San who had just grabbed a cloth and poured something on it before placing it on Wooyoung's face.

Wooyoung feels dizzy, lightheaded, and he squirms under San, San grabs his wrists with one arm and pins them above his head while the other presses the cloth down on Wooyoung's face.
Wooyoung's vision blurs, and the next thing you know he's out like a nightlamp.

 

Light shone directly in Wooyoung's face, he woke up tied to a chair in pure darkness, his body ached and his sweater had been taken off his upper body.
Wooyoung tried rubbing his eyes but his wrists were tied tightly to the armleans of the chair.

He looked down to assess the rest of his body, he'd been trained to do this multiple times during the training periods for entering the law enforcement.

His feet were tied to the legs of the chair, he tried wiggling his foot to find the pocket knife he usually brought with him everywhere.

" Don't bother, I've stripped you of every belonging. " A dark voice echoed out.

The dark voice that belonged to San.

The light shining directly in Wooyoung's face dimmed, and a cheap 90s light flicked on, barely lighting up the cemented room, it looked like it was originally a bomb-shelter room.
San stood off to the left of Wooyoung, next to tables with multiple equipment lined up that Wooyoung couldn't see.

Wooyoung's thoughts raised, where was I? how long was I out? where are my sweater and belongings?

" Don't worry, I won't kill you, atleast not yet. " San spoke softly, coming towards Wooyoung slowly, he held something behind him that Wooyoung couldn't see

" I just want to ask you a few questions. " San spoke again, tilting his head creepily at Wooyoung.

He looked psychotic.

That's when he revealed what he held in his hand when he brought his hand forward.

A shock collar for dogs, controlled with a remote he held in the same hand.

Cool, I'm gonna be shocked, hurray!!

San leaned forward to attach the collar around Wooyoung's neck, it seemed like he'd changed clothes as he now wore a black simple t-shirt with black sweats, Wooyoung took a quick peek at the bandages he'd used on San's arms and it seemed like they'd been taken off and re-bandaged with new bandages. It's good but Wooyoung wondered who, did anyone else take care of San?

Wooyoung felt the collar tighten around his neck, it didn't hurt and it almost seemed like San was making sure it didn't hurt with the gaze he was giving Wooyoung.

San stepped back from Wooyoung, staring down at him with crossed arms and an intense gaze.

" Now, over to the fun part. "

 

READING DISCRETION FOR THE REST OF THE STORY IS ADVISED

 

If Wooyoung were to explain in one word how experiencing what San was doing felt like? Torture, pure Torture.

His wrists gripped the edges of the arms on the chairs tightly, he whimpered, his hair was messy and fluffed up and sweat was rolling down his forehead and they were only 5 minutes in.

San stood there, pressing on the shock button, the remote had several controls.
One button for vibration only to serve as a warning, one button for the intensity of the shocks which was 6 various intensities, (6 being the lethal one),
one for how the shocks would feel;
sometimes it felt like Wooyoung was being struck repeatedly by lightning,
other times it felt like Wooyoung was on fire.
The one used on him the most is where his whole body tenses up as electric bolts are being shot through his entire body from the top of his head to the tip of his big toe.

Wooyoung glared back up at San without saying anything, he was biting his tongue so hard that he swore he could taste metal.

San clenched his jaw and shoved Wooyoung's head back from him, he stepped back and upped the intensity of the shocks, his thumb pressed tightly down on the shock remote causing Wooyoung's back to curve, and his head spinning and his toes curling as the shocks made every single muscle in his body tighten.

Wooyoung managed to somehow endure it, with the training he'd have regularly while being in the police force he managed to man up and take it in silence.

" Fuck. " San whispered under his breath.

The sight of Wooyoung's back arching like that did stuff to San, it was a picture he'd take with him to his grave and he'd never share it with anyone.
No one should see that sight but him.

San spoke in a menacing tone and it almost made Wooyoung laugh. Changbin is smart but he ain't that smart. He's lazy as fuck and he wouldn't lift a finger to look through databases like that, Wooyoung didn't do anything of that sort either but out of the two it was more likely that Wooyoung would be the one to find out about San's sister.

It's giving Fifty shades of grey but oh well.

San turned to look at him, a dark, cold and fierce expression still plastered on his face.

Wooyoung wanted to protect Mingi.
Mingi was way too sweet for this.
He didn't do anything to get ratted out and get his ass kicked again by San.

Did he?

Wooyoung knows how to protect himself, he should be just fine.

He was laying on a couch, in someone's house. Who's though?

Wooyoung looked around, he'd been previously covered with a soft black blanket by someone.

" San? " Wooyoung asked, his voice breaking and hoarse from all the screaming in pain and moaning in pain from the day prior.

earlier. " Wooyoung asked.

knock... knock... knock knock knock..... knock... knock..
